  • 2026-11-10 13:48:09 PT | record | NOTE — DETECTION UPGRADE (effective today): three changes start their own record from today and are never backfilled. (1) E3 CLUSTER FUNDS FRESH WALLET is upgraded: it now fires as an ALERT instead of an INFO, its funding floor is the seed evidence floor of 0.01 SOL instead of 0.05 SOL, and its cooldown is one alert per funding wallet per 6 hours. E3 records before today were made under the old shape. (2) Two new event types begin: E20 CREATOR LAUNCHED NEW COIN and E21 CREATOR TOOK NEW POSITION, both INFO, fired only when the creator paid for the transaction himself, so airdrops and passive receipts never count. (3) Delivered alerts now carry a latency receipt (on chain time, sent time, seconds) whenever the event has a real chain timestamp. Events without one carry no receipt.
  • 2026-11-10 13:48:10 PT | record | NOTE — FUNDING INTELLIGENCE (effective today): three changes start their own record from today and are never backfilled. (1) The system now keeps a permanent funder registry: every classified funder and wallet, from every coin, stays in the database with its evidence, so a pipe or a funder met once is known when it appears again. A fresh-wallet arrival the registry already knows as venue infrastructure no longer fires a whale alert; it is noted as infrastructure instead. (2) When two or more fresh-wallet arrivals land on a coin inside ten minutes, a grouped summary is delivered and logged alongside the individual alerts: combined percent of supply, wallet count, and the funding verdict for each. The grouping is timing and size only; it claims no shared owner. (3) A wallet once reported as funding-unclear can be reclassified, but only by money flow: if a proven cluster wallet later sends it SOL or the coin itself, a follow-up alert (E22) fires with the transaction as evidence. Behavior alone never reclassifies; a synchronized-moves note (E23) may be logged with its interpretation labeled as interpretation, and it changes no classification.
  • 2026-11-10 13:48:12 PT | record | NOTE — NEW EVENT TYPE E24 (CLUSTER WALLET REFILL) LIVE from this restart. It fires when a proven dev cluster wallet that had fully exited a watched coin (balance zero or dust, under 0.001% of supply, on a direct balance read) receives a refill of at least 0.25% of supply, or when an empty cluster wallet that was dry on SOL receives at least 1 SOL. The refill source is classified on money-flow evidence only: another cluster member (named), shared infrastructure, an independent wallet, or unclear. Two direct balance reads are required, one showing empty and one showing loaded, so a single bad read cannot create this event. Where an inflow qualifies as both E24 and E17, E24 is the record and E17 does not double-fire. E24 grades from today. Nothing is backfilled.

One line of separation

TELLS is not BONDED. A TELLS alert says something moved. It says nothing about any bond.

TELLS watches and reports. It does not trade, and it does not promise. Most coins go to zero. A guard is not a guarantee. Back to TELLS.
